{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_9826a412f8ae46199113443f","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Deliver a user's conversation list over the WebSocket protocol\n2\t\n3\tIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#144\n4\tLabels: type:feature, size:M, area:api, area:compliance, epic:extension-points\n5\t\n6\tPart of #35\n7\t\n8\t## Why\n9\t\n10\t#143 gives a consumer `store.listFor(userId)`.\n11\tIt does not give a **client** anything: the browser holding the socket cannot reach the store, so\n12\tevery consumer that wants a history sidebar has to stand up its own authenticated HTTP route over\n13\tthe same data the engine already authenticated on the upgrade. That is a second authorisation\n14\tpath to the same personal data, written once per consumer, each one able to get the ownership\n15\tcheck wrong in its own way — the exact failure #45 went out of its way to avoid for resume.\n16\t\n17\tA consumer may have no such route to extend: an engine deployment behind an api with a session\n18\tstore and no conversation endpoints of its own has nothing to add this to. The engine already knows who is connected and already\n19\treplays a conversation on resume; answering \"which conversations do I have\" over the same socket\n20\tis the smaller surface.\n21\t\n22\t## What to do\n23\t\n24\t- `src/types/messages.ts`, additive only:\n25\t  - `ListConversationsMessage {type: 'list_conversations'; limit?: number; before?: string}` into\n26\t    `IncomingMessage`. There is no user id field, and that is the point: the list is always the\n27\t    connection's authenticated user's.\n28\t  - `ConversationListMessage {type: 'conversation_list'; conversations: ConversationSummary[]}`\n29\t    into `OutgoingMessage`, where `ConversationSummary` is `SessionSummary` with its two times as\n30\t    ISO strings — `Date` does not survive `JSON.stringify` as a `Date`, and the protocol types\n31\t    describe what goes on the wire.\n32\t- `validation.ts`: a `list_conversations` case — `limit`, when present, an integer 1–200;\n33\t  `before`, when present, a string `Date.parse` accepts. Anything else is `INVALID_MESSAGE`, as\n34\t  the two existing cases do it.\n35\t- Handling: `createMessageHandler` takes the session store and the new options alongside the\n36\t  orchestrator (`createServer` already holds both), and answers `list_conversations` on the\n37\t  non-locked path beside `ping` — a history request is not a turn and must not queue behind a\n38\t  streaming answer.\n39\t- The user id comes from `ws.userId`, never from the frame. Add the regression:\n40\t  a frame carrying an extra `userId` of another user is answered with the connecting user's own\n41\t  list.\n42\t- Opt-in: `HalEngineConfig.transport.history?: {enabled: boolean}`, default off, threaded through\n43\t  `createServer`'s options like `resume`. With it off, `list_conversations` is answered with an\n44\t  `error` frame and no list.\n45\t- A store that implements no `listFor` is answered with an `error`, not an empty list: a client\n46\t  that renders \"no conversations\" for a user who has several is a worse answer than one that\n47\t  renders a failure. `ErrorCodes` gains one code for both cases (`UNSUPPORTED`), additive.\n48\t- Picking a conversation needs nothing new: the client reconnects with `?sessionId=<id>`, which\n49\t  #45 already ships with the ownership check and the replay. No in-band switch frame, no second\n50\t  replay path.\n51\t- Specs and docs: extend `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md` (the two frames, their\n52\t  field tables, the authorisation rule) and the conversation-list spec; add § Conversation list\n53\t  to `docs/getting-started.md` by editing `example/full-config.ts` and running `npm run docs:fix`\n54\t  — that listing sits under `<!-- doc-block: example/full-config.ts#full-config -->` and\n55\t  `npm run docs:check` fails a hand-edited block.\n56\t\n57\t## Decisions\n58\t\n59\t- **A frame, not a REST route.** The REST chat routes are demo-only and are not where a consumer is meant to build. The WebSocket is where the caller is\n60\t  already authenticated, once per connection, by the consumer's own `wsAuth`.\n61\t- **Its own opt-in flag, separate from `resume`.** Resume hands a client back a conversation it\n62\t  was already in; a list tells it what else the user has. A deployer who turned on resume did not\n63\t  thereby agree to the second disclosure, and a config that conflates them cannot be audited.\n64\t- **Answered off the per-session lock.** `PerSessionLock` serialises turns (#47). A list request\n65\t  that queued behind a two-minute streaming answer would look broken, and it mutates nothing.\n66\t- **An error, not an empty list, when it cannot be answered.** See above; silence that looks like\n67\t  data is the one failure mode a history view cannot recover from.\n68\t- **Answered when asked, never pushed.** No `conversation_list` arrives unsolicited: a client\n69\t  that just started a conversation knows, and a push would need a fan-out across the sockets a\n70\t  resumed session can be open on.\n71\t- **The frame is accepted by `validateMessage` whether or not the feature is on**, and the\n72\t  handler is what refuses it. Validation is static and has no config; the alternative is\n73\t  threading options into a pure validator to turn a type into \"unknown\".\n74\t\n75\t## Acceptance criteria\n76\t\n77\t- [ ] `src/types/messages.ts` gains only new interfaces and their two union members (**amended:** four interfaces, not two — each frame needs a type for its own payload, `ConversationCursor` for what a client sends back and `ConversationSummary` for a row on the wire) — a\n78\t      `git diff` of that file touches no existing line, and a fixture pins that every message\n79\t      literal valid before this change still compiles\n80\t- [ ] `validateMessage({type: 'list_conversations'})` is valid; with `limit: 0`, `limit: 201`,\n81\t      `limit: 1.5`, `limit: '10'` or `before: 'not a date'` it is invalid with a message naming\n82\t      the field\n83\t- [ ] With `history: {enabled: true}` and a store holding three conversations for the user, a\n84\t      `list_conversations` frame is answered with exactly one `conversation_list` carrying three\n85\t      summaries, newest first, each `createdAt` and `updatedAt` an ISO string, and no\n86\t      `stream_end`\n87\t- [ ] A frame carrying `{type: 'list_conversations', userId: '<another user>'}` is answered with\n88\t      the connecting user's own list, and the store's `listFor` is called with `ws.userId` —\n89\t      asserted on the recorded argument, not only on the frame\n90\t- [ ] `limit` and `before` reach `listFor` as the frame sent them. **Amended:** `before` is a\n91\t      `{updatedAt, sessionId}` cursor of ISO strings, not a `Date`. This was written before\n92\t      #143's review found that paging on a timestamp alone drops a conversation whenever two\n93\t      share an activity time, so the store's cursor became a composite that accepts an ISO\n94\t      string; a `Date` cannot carry the tie-break. The handler passes `{limit, before}` with\n95\t      both possibly `undefined` rather than omitting the argument, which is the same call\n96\t- [ ] With `history` absent, the same frame is answered with one `error` frame whose code is\n97\t      `UNSUPPORTED` and no `conversation_list`\n98\t- [ ] With `history: {enabled: true}` against a store implementing no `listFor`, the answer is\n99\t      the same `error` frame, and `grep` shows no code path that sends `conversations: []`\n100\t- [ ] A `listFor` that rejects is answered with one `error` frame whose code is `SERVER_ERROR`\n101\t      and whose message carries no store detail, the socket stays open, and the failure is\n102\t      logged with the session id and no user content. **Added during the build:** a store throwing\n103\t      the new exported `StoreCannotList` is answered `UNSUPPORTED` instead, so an injected\n104\t      collection without a `find` reads as the misconfiguration it is rather than a database\n105\t      that failed\n106\t- [ ] A `list_conversations` sent while a `user_message` is streaming on the same session is\n107\t      answered before that turn's `stream_end` — it does not wait on the lock\n108\t- [ ] A client that takes an id from a `conversation_list` and reconnects with `?sessionId=<id>`\n109\t      receives `resumed: true` and that conversation's replay, with no new transport code: the\n110\t      test exercises the existing #45 path end to end\n111\t- [ ] `ConnectedMessage` is unchanged by this issue\n112\t- [ ] **GDPR.** The protocol spec states that `conversation_list` carries one authenticated\n113\t      user's own conversation metadata and no conversation content, that the user id is taken\n114\t      from the connection and never from the frame, and that `transport.history` is the\n115\t      deployer's decision to make a user's history reachable from the client; `SessionResumeOptions`'\n116\t      retention note is mirrored on the new options type, pointing at #41\n117\t- [ ] `CHANGELOG.md` carries an entry under `## [Unreleased]` written for somebody installing the\n118\t      package\n119\t- [ ] The `docs/getting-started.md` change is made in `example/full-config.ts` and the doc\n120\t      regenerated with `npm run docs:fix`, not typed into the markdown\n121\t- [ ] Spec statements carry trailing `([validated by: ...](...#Lnn))` citations, `Status` rows\n122\t      match coverage, and `npm run check:spec-links`, `npm run check:spec-status` and\n123\t      `npm run spec:names` pass\n124\t- [ ] The version bump is MINOR — new optional config, two additive protocol frames, one new\n125\t      error code — and the PR body says so; no migration guide is owed\n126\t- [ ] `npm run verify` passes and the coverage floors for the touched groups hold or rise\n127\t\n128\t## Amendments\n129\t\n130\tThree things changed between writing this issue and building it, each marked in the criterion it\n131\taffects:\n132\t\n133\t1. **`before` is a cursor, not a timestamp.** #143's review found that paging on `updatedAt` alone\n134\t   silently drops a conversation when two share an activity time. The store's cursor became\n135\t   `{updatedAt, sessionId}`, so the frame carries both halves and the validator refuses one half\n136\t   alone. Built as originally written, the transport would have reintroduced the defect the store\n137\t   had just been fixed for.\n138\t2. **`messages.ts` gains four interfaces, not two** — the two frames plus the two payload types\n139\t   they describe.\n140\t3. **`StoreCannotList` is new public surface**, beyond what this issue specified: without it a\n141\t   permanent store misconfiguration cannot be told from a transient failure, in either the client\n142\t   frame or the log.\n143\t\n144\tTwo decisions the criteria did not anticipate:\n145\t\n146\t- **`transport.history` without `transport.resume` is useless, and now says so.** `?sessionId=` is\n147\t  read only when resume is enabled, so a list whose rows cannot be opened is a deployment slip:\n148\t  the engine warns at startup and the documentation says so where it tells a client to reconnect\n149\t  with a row's id. Refusing to start was considered and rejected as too blunt for a library.\n150\t- **The conversation the connection is in is listed like any other**, so a fresh connect sees a row\n151\t  of its own holding no entries. Filtering it out was rejected: it is a real conversation the user\n152\t  is in, and it is their newest the moment they send anything.\n153\t\n154\t## Tech notes\n155\t\n156\tDepends on #143: without `listFor` there is\n157\tnothing to send.\n158\t\n159\tKey paths: `src/types/messages.ts`, `src/types/session.ts` (`ErrorCodes`),\n160\t`src/transport/ws/{validation,messageHandler,sender}.ts`, `src/transport/createServer.ts`,\n161\t`src/config.ts`, `example/full-config.ts`, `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md`.\n162\t\n163\tOut of scope: a preview or title on the summaries (its own issue); deleting a conversation from\n164\tthe client, which would put an erasure primitive on the wire and needs its own authorisation\n165\tdecision; paging state held server-side — the client pages with `before`; an operator view of\n166\tanother user's conversations, which is an authorisation question of its own; and live sync between open tabs,\n167\twhich #124 ruled out.\n168\t\n169\tRefs #143, #145, #41, #45, #47, #124\n170\t\n171\t\n172\t\n173\t\n174\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"085f11a5-4b28-4243-909c-453cbeea0f9f","uuid":"bcb92a2b-2a08-42b6-b721-b58468e4a3cf","timestamp":"2026-11-05T20:55:37.411Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/acd5e73f7ba6/workspace/ticket.md","content":"# Deliver a user's conversation list over the WebSocket protocol\n\nIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#144\nLabels: type:feature, size:M, area:api, area:compliance, epic:extension-points\n\nPart of #35\n\n## Why\n\n#143 gives a consumer `store.listFor(userId)`.\nIt does not give a **client** anything: the browser holding the socket cannot reach the store, so\nevery consumer that wants a history sidebar has to stand up its own authenticated HTTP route over\nthe same data the engine already authenticated on the upgrade. That is a second authorisation\npath to the same personal data, written once per consumer, each one able to get the ownership\ncheck wrong in its own way — the exact failure #45 went out of its way to avoid for resume.\n\nA consumer may have no such route to extend: an engine deployment behind an api with a session\nstore and no conversation endpoints of its own has nothing to add this to. The engine already knows who is connected and already\nreplays a conversation on resume; answering \"which conversations do I have\" over the same socket\nis the smaller surface.\n\n## What to do\n\n- `src/types/messages.ts`, additive only:\n  - `ListConversationsMessage {type: 'list_conversations'; limit?: number; before?: string}` into\n    `IncomingMessage`. There is no user id field, and that is the point: the list is always the\n    connection's authenticated user's.\n  - `ConversationListMessage {type: 'conversation_list'; conversations: ConversationSummary[]}`\n    into `OutgoingMessage`, where `ConversationSummary` is `SessionSummary` with its two times as\n    ISO strings — `Date` does not survive `JSON.stringify` as a `Date`, and the protocol types\n    describe what goes on the wire.\n- `validation.ts`: a `list_conversations` case — `limit`, when present, an integer 1–200;\n  `before`, when present, a string `Date.parse` accepts. Anything else is `INVALID_MESSAGE`, as\n  the two existing cases do it.\n- Handling: `createMessageHandler` takes the session store and the new options alongside the\n  orchestrator (`createServer` already holds both), and answers `list_conversations` on the\n  non-locked path beside `ping` — a history request is not a turn and must not queue behind a\n  streaming answer.\n- The user id comes from `ws.userId`, never from the frame. Add the regression:\n  a frame carrying an extra `userId` of another user is answered with the connecting user's own\n  list.\n- Opt-in: `HalEngineConfig.transport.history?: {enabled: boolean}`, default off, threaded through\n  `createServer`'s options like `resume`. With it off, `list_conversations` is answered with an\n  `error` frame and no list.\n- A store that implements no `listFor` is answered with an `error`, not an empty list: a client\n  that renders \"no conversations\" for a user who has several is a worse answer than one that\n  renders a failure. `ErrorCodes` gains one code for both cases (`UNSUPPORTED`), additive.\n- Picking a conversation needs nothing new: the client reconnects with `?sessionId=<id>`, which\n  #45 already ships with the ownership check and the replay. No in-band switch frame, no second\n  replay path.\n- Specs and docs: extend `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md` (the two frames, their\n  field tables, the authorisation rule) and the conversation-list spec; add § Conversation list\n  to `docs/getting-started.md` by editing `example/full-config.ts` and running `npm run docs:fix`\n  — that listing sits under `<!-- doc-block: example/full-config.ts#full-config -->` and\n  `npm run docs:check` fails a hand-edited block.\n\n## Decisions\n\n- **A frame, not a REST route.** The REST chat routes are demo-only and are not where a consumer is meant to build. The WebSocket is where the caller is\n  already authenticated, once per connection, by the consumer's own `wsAuth`.\n- **Its own opt-in flag, separate from `resume`.** Resume hands a client back a conversation it\n  was already in; a list tells it what else the user has. A deployer who turned on resume did not\n  thereby agree to the second disclosure, and a config that conflates them cannot be audited.\n- **Answered off the per-session lock.** `PerSessionLock` serialises turns (#47). A list request\n  that queued behind a two-minute streaming answer would look broken, and it mutates nothing.\n- **An error, not an empty list, when it cannot be answered.** See above; silence that looks like\n  data is the one failure mode a history view cannot recover from.\n- **Answered when asked, never pushed.** No `conversation_list` arrives unsolicited: a client\n  that just started a conversation knows, and a push would need a fan-out across the sockets a\n  resumed session can be open on.\n- **The frame is accepted by `validateMessage` whether or not the feature is on**, and the\n  handler is what refuses it. Validation is static and has no config; the alternative is\n  threading options into a pure validator to turn a type into \"unknown\".\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] `src/types/messages.ts` gains only new interfaces and their two union members (**amended:** four interfaces, not two — each frame needs a type for its own payload, `ConversationCursor` for what a client sends back and `ConversationSummary` for a row on the wire) — a\n      `git diff` of that file touches no existing line, and a fixture pins that every message\n      literal valid before this change still compiles\n- [ ] `validateMessage({type: 'list_conversations'})` is valid; with `limit: 0`, `limit: 201`,\n      `limit: 1.5`, `limit: '10'` or `before: 'not a date'` it is invalid with a message naming\n      the field\n- [ ] With `history: {enabled: true}` and a store holding three conversations for the user, a\n      `list_conversations` frame is answered with exactly one `conversation_list` carrying three\n      summaries, newest first, each `createdAt` and `updatedAt` an ISO string, and no\n      `stream_end`\n- [ ] A frame carrying `{type: 'list_conversations', userId: '<another user>'}` is answered with\n      the connecting user's own list, and the store's `listFor` is called with `ws.userId` —\n      asserted on the recorded argument, not only on the frame\n- [ ] `limit` and `before` reach `listFor` as the frame sent them. **Amended:** `before` is a\n      `{updatedAt, sessionId}` cursor of ISO strings, not a `Date`. This was written before\n      #143's review found that paging on a timestamp alone drops a conversation whenever two\n      share an activity time, so the store's cursor became a composite that accepts an ISO\n      string; a `Date` cannot carry the tie-break. The handler passes `{limit, before}` with\n      both possibly `undefined` rather than omitting the argument, which is the same call\n- [ ] With `history` absent, the same frame is answered with one `error` frame whose code is\n      `UNSUPPORTED` and no `conversation_list`\n- [ ] With `history: {enabled: true}` against a store implementing no `listFor`, the answer is\n      the same `error` frame, and `grep` shows no code path that sends `conversations: []`\n- [ ] A `listFor` that rejects is answered with one `error` frame whose code is `SERVER_ERROR`\n      and whose message carries no store detail, the socket stays open, and the failure is\n      logged with the session id and no user content. **Added during the build:** a store throwing\n      the new exported `StoreCannotList` is answered `UNSUPPORTED` instead, so an injected\n      collection without a `find` reads as the misconfiguration it is rather than a database\n      that failed\n- [ ] A `list_conversations` sent while a `user_message` is streaming on the same session is\n      answered before that turn's `stream_end` — it does not wait on the lock\n- [ ] A client that takes an id from a `conversation_list` and reconnects with `?sessionId=<id>`\n      receives `resumed: true` and that conversation's replay, with no new transport code: the\n      test exercises the existing #45 path end to end\n- [ ] `ConnectedMessage` is unchanged by this issue\n- [ ] **GDPR.** The protocol spec states that `conversation_list` carries one authenticated\n      user's own conversation metadata and no conversation content, that the user id is taken\n      from the connection and never from the frame, and that `transport.history` is the\n      deployer's decision to make a user's history reachable from the client; `SessionResumeOptions`'\n      retention note is mirrored on the new options type, pointing at #41\n- [ ] `CHANGELOG.md` carries an entry under `## [Unreleased]` written for somebody installing the\n      package\n- [ ] The `docs/getting-started.md` change is made in `example/full-config.ts` and the doc\n      regenerated with `npm run docs:fix`, not typed into the markdown\n- [ ] Spec statements carry trailing `([validated by: ...](...#Lnn))` citations, `Status` rows\n      match coverage, and `npm run check:spec-links`, `npm run check:spec-status` and\n      `npm run spec:names` pass\n- [ ] The version bump is MINOR — new optional config, two additive protocol frames, one new\n      error code — and the PR body says so; no migration guide is owed\n- [ ] `npm run verify` passes and the coverage floors for the touched groups hold or rise\n\n## Amendments\n\nThree things changed between writing this issue and building it, each marked in the criterion it\naffects:\n\n1. **`before` is a cursor, not a timestamp.** #143's review found that paging on `updatedAt` alone\n   silently drops a conversation when two share an activity time. The store's cursor became\n   `{updatedAt, sessionId}`, so the frame carries both halves and the validator refuses one half\n   alone. Built as originally written, the transport would have reintroduced the defect the store\n   had just been fixed for.\n2. **`messages.ts` gains four interfaces, not two** — the two frames plus the two payload types\n   they describe.\n3. **`StoreCannotList` is new public surface**, beyond what this issue specified: without it a\n   permanent store misconfiguration cannot be told from a transient failure, in either the client\n   frame or the log.\n\nTwo decisions the criteria did not anticipate:\n\n- **`transport.history` without `transport.resume` is useless, and now says so.** `?sessionId=` is\n  read only when resume is enabled, so a list whose rows cannot be opened is a deployment slip:\n  the engine warns at startup and the documentation says so where it tells a client to reconnect\n  with a row's id. Refusing to start was considered and rejected as too blunt for a library.\n- **The conversation the connection is in is listed like any other**, so a fresh connect sees a row\n  of its own holding no entries. Filtering it out was rejected: it is a real conversation the user\n  is in, and it is their newest the moment they send anything.\n\n## Tech notes\n\nDepends on #143: without `listFor` there is\nnothing to send.\n\nKey paths: `src/types/messages.ts`, `src/types/session.ts` (`ErrorCodes`),\n`src/transport/ws/{validation,messageHandler,sender}.ts`, `src/transport/createServer.ts`,\n`src/config.ts`, `example/full-config.ts`, `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md`.\n\nOut of scope: a preview or title on the summaries (its own issue); deleting a conversation from\nthe client, which would put an erasure primitive on the wire and needs its own authorisation\ndecision; paging state held server-side — the client pages with `before`; an operator view of\nanother user's conversations, which is an authorisation question of its own; and live sync between open tabs,\nwhich #124 ruled out.\n\nRefs #143, #145, #41, #45, #47, #124\n\n\n\n\n","numLines":174,"startLine":1,"totalLines":174}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_9826a412f8ae46199113443f","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

sessions}` with `createdAt` and `updatedAt` serialised as ISO-8601 strings.\n  `src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **listing conversations over the WebSocket protocol › answers an empty session_list when the store has no listFor, and SERVER_ERROR with a ws error log when listFor throws** — Verifies that a store without `listFor` answers `{type: 'session_list', sessions: []}` and a store whose `listFor` throws/rejects logs an error under category `'ws'` with the userId and error message and answers `{type: 'error', code: 'SERVER_ERROR'}`.\n  `src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts`\n\n- [ ] **listing conversations over the WebSocket protocol › accepts a bare list_sessions frame and rejects a malformed limit or before with INVALID_MESSAGE without calling listFor** — Verifies that `{type: 'list_sessions'}` is accepted while non-integer or negative `limit` values and malformed `before` cursors (`sessionId` outside 1–128 {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_45e882596f1f44d8a2d1e418","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\n2\timport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\n3\timport type {Express} from 'express';\n4\timport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\n5\timport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\n6\timport {answeringOrchestrator} from './wsTestSupport.js';\n7\timport {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\n8\timport type {SessionCursor, SessionListOptions, SessionStore, SessionSummary} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n9\timport {setLogger} from '../shared/logger.js';\n10\t\n11\t// Real upgrades over a real socket: what the list_sessions frame answers is the contract, and no double can observe it.\n12\t\n13\tinterface Frame {\n14\t  type: string;\n15\t  sessionId?: string;\n16\t  sessions?: Array<{sessionId: string; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; entryCount: number}>;\n17\t  code?: string;\n18\t  message?: string;\n19\t}\n20\t\n21\tconst servers: Array<ReturnType<typeof createServer>> = [];\n22\tconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n23\tconst logLines: Array<{level: string; category: string; message: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown>}> = [];\n24\t\n25\tconst eventually = async (check: () => boolean): Promise<void> => {\n26\t  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50 && !check(); attempt++) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));\n27\t};\n28\t\n29\tbeforeEach(() => {\n30\t  logLines.length = 0;\n31\t  const record = (level: string) => (category: string, message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>) =>\n32\t    void logLines.push({level, category, message, fields});\n33\t  setLogger({debug: record('debug'), info: record('info'), warn: record('warn'), error: record('error')});\n34\t});\n35\t\n36\tafterEach(async () => {\n37\t  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n38\t  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) {\n39\t    const {wss} = hal;\n40\t    await eventually(() => wss.clients.size === 0);\n41\t    await hal.stop();\n42\t  }\n43\t  setLogger();\n44\t});\n45\t\n46\tconst startEngine = async (store: SessionStore, userId: string | number = 'u1') => {\n47\t  const hal = createServer({\n48\t    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n49\t    wsAuth: async () => ({id: userId}),\n50\t    sessionStore: store,\n51\t    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n52\t  });\n53\t  await hal.start(0);\n54\t  servers.push(hal);\n55\t  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n56\t  return `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws`;\n57\t};\n58\t\n59\tconst askAfterConnect = async (url: string, outbound: unknown): Promise<{connected: Frame; frames: Frame[]}> => {\n60\t  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, 't']);\n61\t  clients.push(client);\n62\t  const frames: Frame[] = [];\n63\t  let connected: Frame = {type: ''};\n64\t  await new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n65\t    client.on('message', raw => {\n66\t      const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n67\t      if (frame.type === 'connected') {\n68\t        connected = frame;\n69\t        client.send(typeof outbound === 'string' ? outbound : JSON.stringify(outbound));\n70\t        client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1}));\n71\t        return;\n72\t      }\n73\t      if (frame.type === 'pong') {\n74\t        resolve();\n75\t        return;\n76\t      }\n77\t      frames.push(frame);\n78\t    });\n79\t  });\n80\t  return {connected, frames};\n81\t};\n82\t\n83\t// A store with only the five required members, so listFor can be omitted, recorded or made to throw.\n84\tconst delegatingTo = (inner: InMemorySessionStore): SessionStore => ({\n85\t  create: (sessionId, userId, options) => inner.create(sessionId, userId, options),\n86\t  get: sessionId => inner.get(sessionId),\n87\t  delete: sessionId => inner.delete(sessionId),\n88\t  count: () => inner.count(),\n89\t  clear: () => inner.clear(),\n90\t});\n91\t\n92\tdescribe('listing conversations over the WebSocket protocol', () => {\n93\t  it('answers list_sessions for the authenticated user with ISO dates, forwarding limit and before while ignoring any client userId', async () => {\n94\t    const inner = new InMemorySessionStore();\n95\t    const seen: Array<{userId: type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\nimport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport {answeringOrchestrator} from './wsTestSupport.js';\nimport {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\nimport type {SessionCursor, SessionListOptions, SessionStore, SessionSummary} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {setLogger} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\n// Real upgrades over a real socket: what the list_sessions frame answers is the contract, and no double can observe it.\n\ninterface Frame {\n  type: string;\n  sessionId?: string;\n  sessions?: Array<{sessionId: string; createdAt: string; updatedAt: string; entryCount: number}>;\n  code?: string;\n  message?: string;\n}\n\nconst servers: Array<ReturnType<typeof createServer>> = [];\nconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\nconst logLines: Array<{level: string; category: string; message: string; fields?: Record<string, unknown>}> = [];\n\nconst eventually = async (check: () => boolean): Promise<void> => {\n  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50 && !check(); attempt++) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));\n};\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  logLines.length = 0;\n  const record = (level: string) => (category: string, message: string, fields?: Record<string, unknown>) =>\n    void logLines.push({level, category, message, fields});\n  setLogger({debug: record('debug'), info: record('info'), warn: record('warn'), error: record('error')});\n});\n\nafterEach(async () => {\n  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) {\n    const {wss} = hal;\n    await eventually(() => wss.clients.size === 0);\n    await hal.stop();\n  }\n  setLogger();\n});\n\nconst startEngine = async (store: SessionStore, userId: string | number = 'u1') => {\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: userId}),\n    sessionStore: store,\n    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws`;\n};\n\nconst askAfterConnect = async (url: string, outbound: unknown): Promise<{connected: Frame; frames: Frame[]}> => {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, 't']);\n  clients.push(client);\n  const frames: Frame[] = [];\n  let connected: Frame = {type: ''};\n  await new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') {\n        connected = frame;\n        client.send(typeof outbound === 'string' ? outbound : JSON.stringify(outbound));\n        client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1}));\n        return;\n      }\n      if (frame.type === 'pong') {\n        resolve();\n        return;\n      }\n      frames.push(frame);\n    });\n  });\n  return {connected, frames};\n};\n\n// A store with only the five required members, so listFor can be omitted, recorded or made to throw.\nconst delegatingTo = (inner: InMemorySessionStore): SessionStore => ({\n  create: (sessionId, userId, options) => inner.create(sessionId, userId, options),\n  get: sessionId => inner.get(sessionId),\n  delete: sessionId => inner.delete(sessionId),\n  count: () => inner.count(),\n  clear: () => inner.clear(),\n});\n\ndescribe('listing conversations over the WebSocket protocol', () => {\n  it('answers list_sessions for the authenticated user with ISO dates, forwarding limit and before while ignoring any client userId', async () => {\n    const inner = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const seen: Array<{userId: string | number; options?: SessionListOptions}> = [];\n    const summaries: SessionSummary[] = [\n      {\n        sessionId: 's2',\n        createdAt: new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),\n        updatedAt: new Date('2026-01-01T00:02:00.000Z'),\n        entryCount: 4,\n      },\n    ];\n    const store: SessionStore = {\n      ...delegatingTo(inner),\n      listFor: (userId, options) => {\n        seen.push({userId, options});\n        return summaries;\n      },\n    };\n    const url = await startEngine(store, 'u1');\n    const before: SessionCursor = {updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:05:00.000Z', sessionId: 's3'};\n\n    const {frames} = await askAfterConnect(url, {type: 'list_sessions', limit: 10, before, userId: 'u2'});\n\n    expect({seen, frames}).toEqual({\n      seen: [{userId: 'u1', options: {limit: 10, before}}],\n      frames: [\n        {\n          type: 'session_list',\n          sessions: [\n            {\n              sessionId: 's2',\n              createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',\n              updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:02:00.000Z',\n              entryCount: 4,\n            },\n          ],\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('answers an empty session_list when the store has no listFor, and SERVER_ERROR with a ws error log when listFor throws', async () => {\n    const inner = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const withoutList = await startEngine(delegatingTo(inner));\n    const failingList = await startEngine({\n      ...delegatingTo(inner),\n      listFor: () => Promise.reject(new Error('database offline')),\n    });\n\n    const unimplemented = await askAfterConnect(withoutList, {type: 'list_sessions'});\n    const failed = await askAfterConnect(failingList, {type: 'list_sessions'});\n\n    expect({\n      unimplemented: unimplemented.frames,\n      failed: failed.frames.map(frame => ({type: frame.type, code: frame.code})),\n      logged: logLines.filter(line => line.level === 'error' && line.category === 'ws'),\n    }).toEqual({\n      unimplemented: [{type: 'session_list', sessions: []}],\n      failed: [{type: 'error', code: 'SERVER_ERROR'}],\n      logged: [\n        {\n          level: 'error',\n          category: 'ws',\n          message: expect.any(String),\n          fields: expect.objectContaining({userId: 'u1', error: 'database offline'}),\n        },\n      ],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('accepts a bare list_sessions frame and rejects a malformed limit or before with INVALID_MESSAGE without calling listFor', async () => {\n    const inner = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    let calls = 0;\n    const store: SessionStore = {\n      ...delegatingTo(inner),\n      listFor: () => {\n        calls += 1;\n        return [];\n      },\n    };\n    const url = await startEngine(store);\n    const badFrames = [\n      {type: 'list_sessions', limit: 'ten'},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', limit: -1},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', limit: 1.5},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', before: 'not-an-object'},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', before: {updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', sessionId: ''}},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', before: {updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z', sessionId: 'a'.repeat(129)}},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', before: {updatedAt: 'not-a-date', sessionId: 's1'}},\n      {type: 'list_sessions', before: {updatedAt: true, sessionId: 's1'}},\n    ];\n\n    const codes: Array<string | undefined> = [];\n    for (const bad of badFrames) {\n      const {frames} = await askAfterConnect(url, bad);\n      codes.push(frames[0]?.code);\n    }\n    const {frames: accepted} = await askAfterConnect(url, {type: 'list_sessions'});\n\n    expect({codes, accepted, calls}).toEqual({\n      codes: Array(badFrames.length).fill('INVALID_MESSAGE'),\n      accepted: [{type: 'session_list', sessions: []}],\n      calls: 1,\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":199,"startLine":1,"totalLines":199}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_45e882596f1f44d8a2d1e418","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_d5e068f148234dbfbbc70f71","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\n2\timport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\n3\timport type {ChatSession} from '../../types/session.js';\n4\timport {ErrorCodes} from '../../types/messages.js';\n5\timport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\n6\timport {ThinkingTagParser, ParsedSegment} from '../../infrastructure/parsers/thinkingTagParser.js';\n7\timport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\n8\timport type {ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\n9\timport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\n10\timport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\n11\timport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\n12\timport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\n13\timport {\n14\t  createUserEntry,\n15\t  createAssistantEntry,\n16\t  createThinkingEntry,\n17\t  createToolEntry,\n18\t} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\n19\timport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\n20\timport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\n21\timport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n22\t\n23\tinterface StreamState {\n24\t  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n25\t  assistantIndex: number | null;\n26\t  suppressOutput: boolean;\n27\t  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\ttype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\n31\ttype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\n32\texport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n33\t  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n34\t  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n35\t  return function handleMessage(\n36\t    ws: WebSocket,\n37\t    session: ChatSession,\n38\t    rawMessage: unknown,\n39\t    signal?: AbortSignal\n40\t  ): Promise<void> {\n41\t    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n42\t    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n43\t    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n44\t      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n45\t    }\n46\t    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n47\t    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n48\t  };\n49\t}\n50\t\n51\t// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\n52\tasync function answerInTurn(\n53\t  ws: WebSocket,\n54\t  session: ChatSession,\n55\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n56\t  validation: ValidationResult,\n57\t  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n58\t): Promise<void> {\n59\t  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n60\t  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n61\t    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n62\t    return;\n63\t  }\n64\t  const startedAt = Date.now();\n65\t  try {\n66\t    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n67\t  } finally {\n68\t    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n69\t  }\n70\t}\n71\t\n72\t// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\n73\tfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n74\t  if (signal?.aborted) {\n75\t    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n76\t      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n77\t      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n78\t    });\n79\t    return;\n80\t  }\n81\t  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\tasync function answerUserMessage(\n85\t  ws: WebSocket,\n86\t  session: ChatSession,\n87\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n88\t  validation: ValidationResult,\n89\t  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n90\t): Promise<void> {\n91\t  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n92\t  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n93\t  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\tfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n97\t  if (!validation.valid) {\n98\t    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n99\t    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n100\t    return;\n101\t  }\n102\t\n103\t  const message = validation.data;\n104\t  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n105\t}\n106\t\n107\t// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\n108\tfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n109\t  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\tasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n113\t  try {\n114\t    await action();\n115\t  } catch (error) {\n116\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n117\t    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n118\t    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n119\t    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n120\t      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n121\t      return;\n122\t    }\n123\t    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n124\t  }\n125\t}\n126\t\n127\tasync function handleUserMessage(\n128\t  ws: WebSocket,\n129\t  session: ChatSession,\n130\t  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n131\t  content: string,\n132\t  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n133\t): Promise<void> {\n134\t  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n135\t\n136\t  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n137\t  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n138\t  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n139\t\n140\t  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n141\t  const state: StreamState = {\n142\t    thinkingIndex: null,\n143\t    assistantIndex: null,\n144\t    suppressOutput: false,\n145\t    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n146\t  };\n147\t\n148\t  let textChunkCount = 0;\n149\t\n150\t  try {\n151\t    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n152\t      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n153\t      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n154\t      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n155\t      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n156\t    }\n157\t  } finally {\n158\t    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n159\t    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n160\t    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n161\t    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n162\t  }\n163\t\n164\t  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n165\t    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n166\t  }\n167\t  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n168\t}\n169\t\n170\t// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\n171\tfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n172\t  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n173\t\n174\t  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n175\t    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n176\t  }\n177\t\n178\t  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n179\t    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n180\t      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n181\t      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n182\t    });\n183\t    return 0;\n184\t  }\n185\t\n186\t  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n187\t  return 0;\n188\t}\n189\t\n190\tfunction processChunk(\n191\t  ws: WebSocket,\n192\t  session: ChatSession,\n193\t  state: StreamState,\n194\t  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n195\t  chunk: MessageChunk\n196\t): void {\n197\t  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n198\t  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n199\t  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n200\t  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n201\t  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n202\t}\n203\t\n204\tfunction processTextChunk(\n205\t  ws: WebSocket,\n206\t  session: ChatSession,\n207\t  state: StreamState,\n208\t  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n209\t  text: string\n210\t): void {\n211\t  const segments = parser.push(text);\n212\t  for (const segment of segments) {\n213\t    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n214\t  }\n215\t}\n216\t\n217\tfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n218\t  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n219\t    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n220\t    return;\n221\t  }\n222\t  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n223\t  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n224\t}\n225\t\n226\tfunction streamSegment(\n227\t  ws: WebSocket,\n228\t  session: ChatSession,\n229\t  state: StreamState,\n230\t  key: StateIndexKey,\n231\t  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n232\t  content: string\n233\t): void {\n234\t  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n235\t  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n236\t  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n237\t  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n238\t  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n239\t}\n240\t\n241\t// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../../types/session.js';\nimport {ErrorCodes} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport {ThinkingTagParser, ParsedSegment} from '../../infrastructure/parsers/thinkingTagParser.js';\nimport {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\nimport type {ValidationResult} from './validation.js';\nimport {PerSessionLock} from '../../shared/perSessionLock.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../../types/ai.js';\nimport {\n  createUserEntry,\n  createAssistantEntry,\n  createThinkingEntry,\n  createToolEntry,\n} from '../../orchestration/entryFactories.js';\nimport {appendEntry, appendDelta, commitEntry, markTruncated} from '../../orchestration/entryMutations.js';\nimport {sendJson, sendUpsert, sendDelta, sendCommit, sendSkip, sendError, sendStreamEnd} from './sender.js';\nimport {log} from '../../shared/logger.js';\n\ninterface StreamState {\n  thinkingIndex: number | null;\n  assistantIndex: number | null;\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n  sentAssistantIndices: number[];\n}\n\ntype StateIndexKey = 'thinkingIndex' | 'assistantIndex';\ntype EntryFactory = typeof createThinkingEntry | typeof createAssistantEntry;\nexport function createMessageHandler(orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator) {\n  // Keyed by session, not socket: a resumed session can be open on more than one (specs/hal-engine-architecture/spec.md).\n  const lock = new PerSessionLock();\n  return function handleMessage(\n    ws: WebSocket,\n    session: ChatSession,\n    rawMessage: unknown,\n    signal?: AbortSignal\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const validation = validateMessage(rawMessage);\n    // Queued with no await before it, so a session's user_messages, a failed one too, are answered in arrival order.\n    if (isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage)) {\n      return lock.run(session.sessionId, () => answerInTurn(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n    }\n    // An async action runs up to its first await at once, so a ping is answered now and a throw stays a rejection.\n    return wsErrorHandler(ws, async () => answerFrame(ws, validation));\n  };\n}\n\n// The whole answer, an error and the stream_end included, goes out inside the lock: an error frame names no message.\nasync function answerInTurn(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  // Its socket closed while it waited: nobody is left to read the answer, and a session's last close took its credentials.\n  if (signal?.aborted || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSING || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CLOSED) {\n    log.info('message', 'queued message dropped: its socket closed', {sessionId: session.sessionId});\n    return;\n  }\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  try {\n    await wsErrorHandler(ws, () => answerUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, validation, signal));\n  } finally {\n    endTurn(ws, session, signal, startedAt);\n  }\n}\n\n// A turn whose socket closed is abandoned rather than ended: nobody is left to read a terminal frame.\nfunction endTurn(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, signal: AbortSignal | undefined, startedAt: number): void {\n  if (signal?.aborted) {\n    log.info('message', 'turn abandoned: its socket closed', {\n      sessionId: session.sessionId,\n      elapsedMs: Date.now() - startedAt,\n    });\n    return;\n  }\n  sendStreamEnd(ws);\n}\n\nasync function answerUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  validation: ValidationResult,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  const message = validation.valid ? validation.data : undefined;\n  if (message?.type === 'user_message') return handleUserMessage(ws, session, orchestrator, message.content, signal);\n  answerFrame(ws, validation);\n}\n\nfunction answerFrame(ws: WebSocket, validation: ValidationResult): void {\n  if (!validation.valid) {\n    log.warn('message', 'validation failed', {error: validation.error});\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.INVALID_MESSAGE, validation.error);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const message = validation.data;\n  if (message.type === 'ping') handlePing(ws, message.timestamp);\n}\n\n// Read from the raw frame, so a user_message that fails validation still gets its stream_end.\nfunction isUserMessageFrame(rawMessage: unknown): boolean {\n  return typeof rawMessage === 'object' && rawMessage !== null && Reflect.get(rawMessage, 'type') === 'user_message';\n}\n\nasync function wsErrorHandler(ws: WebSocket, action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await action();\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';\n    const code = error instanceof AIError ? error.code : 'UNKNOWN';\n    log.error('message', 'handling error', {error: message, code});\n    if (error instanceof AIError && error.code === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n      sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.RATE_LIMITED, 'AI service is busy, please try again');\n      return;\n    }\n    sendError(ws, ErrorCodes.SERVER_ERROR, 'Failed to process message');\n  }\n}\n\nasync function handleUserMessage(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator,\n  content: string,\n  signal: AbortSignal | undefined\n): Promise<void> {\n  log.info('message', 'user message received', {sessionId: session.sessionId, contentLength: content.length});\n\n  const userEntry = createUserEntry(content);\n  const userIndex = appendEntry(session, userEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, userIndex, userEntry);\n\n  const parser = new ThinkingTagParser();\n  const state: StreamState = {\n    thinkingIndex: null,\n    assistantIndex: null,\n    suppressOutput: false,\n    sentAssistantIndices: [],\n  };\n\n  let textChunkCount = 0;\n\n  try {\n    for await (const chunk of orchestrator.processMessageStream(session, {signal})) {\n      // Its socket closed: breaking returns the run's generator, so the run stops and its own teardown still runs.\n      if (signal?.aborted) break;\n      textChunkCount = logChunk(chunk, textChunkCount);\n      processChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    // Anything still open here was cut short: a stop has already flushed the parser and committed every entry it opened.\n    flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n    closeCutOff(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n  }\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n  log.info('stream', 'stream completed', {suppressed: state.suppressOutput, entries: session.entries.length});\n}\n\n// Diagnostics only: counts a run of text chunks so it is logged once, not per token.\nfunction logChunk(chunk: MessageChunk, textChunkCount: number): number {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return textChunkCount + 1;\n\n  if (textChunkCount > 0) {\n    log.debug('stream', `received ${textChunkCount} text chunks`);\n  }\n\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') {\n    log.debug('stream', 'tool_result chunk', {\n      messageCount: chunk.clientMessages.length,\n      types: chunk.clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n    });\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  log.debug('stream', 'chunk', {type: chunk.type});\n  return 0;\n}\n\nfunction processChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  chunk: MessageChunk\n): void {\n  if (chunk.type === 'text') return processTextChunk(ws, session, state, parser, chunk.text);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_use') return processToolUseChunk(ws, session, chunk.toolCall);\n  if (chunk.type === 'tool_result') return processToolResultChunk(ws, chunk.clientMessages);\n  if (chunk.type === 'suppress_output') return processSuppressChunk(ws, session, state);\n  if (chunk.type === 'stop') return processStopChunk(ws, session, state, parser);\n}\n\nfunction processTextChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  parser: ThinkingTagParser,\n  text: string\n): void {\n  const segments = parser.push(text);\n  for (const segment of segments) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction handleTextSegment(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, segment: ParsedSegment): void {\n  if (segment.type === 'thinking') {\n    streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex', createThinkingEntry, segment.content);\n    return;\n  }\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  streamSegment(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex', createAssistantEntry, segment.content);\n}\n\nfunction streamSegment(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  state: StreamState,\n  key: StateIndexKey,\n  createEntry: EntryFactory,\n  content: string\n): void {\n  const isNew = initSegment(session, state, key, createEntry);\n  if (isNew && state.suppressOutput) skipSuppressed(ws, session, state[key]!);\n  if (isNew && !state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, state[key]!, session.entries[state[key]!]);\n  appendDelta(session, state[key]!, content);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendDelta(ws, state[key]!, content);\n}\n\n// Stored as suppressed, so a replay on resume shows the client what it saw live and not the text a tool hid.\nfunction skipSuppressed(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  markSuppressed(session, index);\n  sendSkip(ws, index);\n}\n\nfunction markSuppressed(session: ChatSession, index: number): void {\n  const entry = session.entries[index];\n  if (entry.role === 'assistant' || entry.role === 'thinking') entry.suppressed = true;\n}\n\nfunction initSegment(session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey, createEntry: EntryFactory): boolean {\n  if (state[key] !== null) return false;\n  state[key] = appendEntry(session, createEntry());\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction commitAndClear(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n\n  commitEntry(session, index);\n  state[key] = null;\n\n  // Only what the client received can be retracted, and only assistant entries.\n  if (state.suppressOutput) return;\n\n  sendCommit(ws, index);\n  if (key === 'assistantIndex') state.sentAssistantIndices.push(index);\n}\n\nfunction processToolUseChunk(\n  ws: WebSocket,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  toolCall: {name: string; input: Record<string, unknown>}\n): void {\n  const toolEntry = createToolEntry(toolCall.name, toolCall.input);\n  const toolIndex = appendEntry(session, toolEntry);\n  sendUpsert(ws, toolIndex, toolEntry);\n}\n\nfunction processToolResultChunk(ws: WebSocket, clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[]): void {\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    log.debug('stream', 'forwarding client message to frontend', {\n      type: msg.type,\n      index: 'index' in msg ? msg.index : undefined,\n    });\n    sendJson(ws, msg);\n  }\n}\n\n// Retracts what the client saw: every assistant entry sent so far is re-sent blank.\nfunction processSuppressChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  // Closed as streamed so far: an open answer is then retracted like a sent one, and what follows opens new entries.\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n  log.debug('stream', 'suppression activated', {blankingEntries: state.sentAssistantIndices});\n\n  for (const idx of state.sentAssistantIndices) {\n    sendUpsert(ws, idx, {...session.entries[idx], content: ''} as SessionEntry);\n    markSuppressed(session, idx);\n  }\n\n  state.sentAssistantIndices = [];\n  state.suppressOutput = true;\n}\n\nfunction processStopChunk(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  flushHeldText(ws, session, state, parser);\n  commitOpenEntries(ws, session, state);\n}\n\n// The parser holds back a tail that might open a tag; without this the tail of a cut-off answer would be lost.\nfunction flushHeldText(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, parser: ThinkingTagParser): void {\n  for (const segment of parser.flush()) {\n    handleTextSegment(ws, session, state, segment);\n  }\n}\n\n// The flag lives on the stored entry even when suppressed; only a visible entry is re-sent, which is how a live client learns it.\nfunction closeCutOff(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState, key: StateIndexKey): void {\n  const index = state[key];\n  if (index === null) return;\n  markTruncated(session, index);\n  if (!state.suppressOutput) sendUpsert(ws, index, session.entries[index]);\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, key);\n}\n\nfunction commitOpenEntries(ws: WebSocket, session: ChatSession, state: StreamState): void {\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'thinkingIndex');\n  commitAndClear(ws, session, state, 'assistantIndex');\n}\n\nfunction handlePing(ws: WebSocket, timestamp: number): void {\n  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'pong', timestamp}));\n}\n","numLines":336,"startLine":1,"totalLines":336}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_d5e068f148234dbfbbc70f71","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

type: 'ping',\n75\t      timestamp: message.timestamp,\n76\t    },\n77\t  };\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\tfunction validateListSessionsMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n81\t  const frame: ListSessionsMessage = {type: 'list_sessions'};\n82\t\n83\t  if (message.limit !== undefined) {\n84\t    if (typeof message.limit !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(message.limit) || validatedCursor(value: unknown): SessionCursor | string {\n101\t  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {\n102\t    return 'list_sessions before must be a {updatedAt, sessionId} type {IncomingMessage, ListSessionsMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionCursor} from '../../types/sessionStore.js';\n\nconst MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = 10000;\nconst MAX_CURSOR_SESSION_ID_LENGTH = 128;\n\ninterface ValidationSuccess {\n  valid: true;\n  data: IncomingMessage;\n}\n\ninterface ValidationFailure {\n  valid: false;\n  error: string;\n}\n\nexport type ValidationResult = ValidationSuccess | ValidationFailure;\n\nexport function validateMessage(payload: unknown): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof payload !== 'object' || payload === null) {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message must be an object'};\n  }\n\n  const message = payload as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n  if (typeof message.type !== 'string') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message must have a type field'};\n  }\n\n  switch (message.type) {\n    case 'user_message':\n      return validateUserMessage(message);\n    case 'ping':\n      return validatePingMessage(message);\n    case 'list_sessions':\n      return validateListSessionsMessage(message);\n    default:\n      return {valid: false, error: `Unknown message type: ${message.type}`};\n  }\n}\n\nfunction validateUserMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof message.content !== 'string') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'user_message must have content string'};\n  }\n\n  const trimmed = message.content.trim();\n\n  if (trimmed.length === 0) {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message content cannot be empty'};\n  }\n\n  if (message.content.length > MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH) {\n    return {valid: false, error: `Message content too long (max ${MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH} chars)`};\n  }\n\n  return {\n    valid: true,\n    data: {\n      type: 'user_message',\n      content: message.content,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction validatePingMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof message.timestamp !== 'number') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'ping must have timestamp number'};\n  }\n\n  return {\n    valid: true,\n    data: {\n      type: 'ping',\n      timestamp: message.timestamp,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction validateListSessionsMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n  const frame: ListSessionsMessage = {type: 'list_sessions'};\n\n  if (message.limit !== undefined) {\n    if (typeof message.limit !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(message.limit) || message.limit < 0) {\n      return {valid: false, error: 'list_sessions limit must be a non-negative integer'};\n    }\n    frame.limit = message.limit;\n  }\n\n  if (message.before !== undefined) {\n    const cursor = validatedCursor(message.before);\n    if (typeof cursor === 'string') return {valid: false, error: cursor};\n    frame.before = cursor;\n  }\n\n  return {valid: true, data: frame};\n}\n\n// Only what the store pages on comes back, so a client cannot smuggle a field into the listFor call.\nfunction validatedCursor(value: unknown): SessionCursor | string {\n  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {\n    return 'list_sessions before must be a {updatedAt, sessionId} cursor';\n  }\n\n  const cursor = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n  const sessionId = cursor.sessionId;\n  if (typeof sessionId !== 'string' || sessionId.length < 1 || sessionId.length > MAX_CURSOR_SESSION_ID_LENGTH) {\n    return 'list_sessions before must carry a sessionId of 1 to 128 characters';\n  }\n\n  const updatedAt = cursor.updatedAt;\n  if (!carriesAMoment(updatedAt)) return 'list_sessions before must carry an updatedAt that parses as a timestamp';\n\n  return {updatedAt: updatedAt as string | number, sessionId};\n}\n\nfunction carriesAMoment(value: unknown): boolean {\n  if (typeof value === 'number') return Number.isFinite(value);\n  return typeof value === 'string' && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));\n}\n","numLines":121,"startLine":1,"totalLines":121}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_a8d4b747db084eb3aa1b18d7","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

'/hal';\n37\t\n38\t  const wss = new WebSocketServer({\n39\t    noServer: true,\n40\t    handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,\n41\t  });\n42\t\n43\t  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);\n44\t  const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? (Number(process.env.PORT) || 8086);\n101\t\n102\t        // Without this an EADDRINUSE settles nothing and takes the process down as an unhandled event.\n103\t        const onError = (error: Error) => reject(error);\n104\t        server.once('error', onError);\n105\t\n106\t        // A listener left from a previous start would report this bind failure as a running server breaking.\n107\t        server.removeListener('error', logLateError);\n108\t\n109\t        server.listen(p, () => {\n110\t          server.removeListener('error', onError);\n111\t\n112\t          // Past the listen window nothing is listening, and an 'error' with no handler ends the process.\n113\t          server.on('error', logLateError);\n114\t\n115\t          // The bound port, not the requested one: a configured 0 means the OS chose it.\n116\t          log.info('server', 'HAL Engine started', {port: boundPort(server, p)});\n117\t          resolve();\n118\t        });\n119\t      }),\n120\t    stop: () =>\n121\t      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n122\t        wss.clients.forEach(ws => ws.close(1001, 'Server shutting down'));\n123\t        wss.close();\n124\t        clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);\n125\t\n126\t        // Nothing to close after a refused start or a second stop: `close` would reject for a server that never ran.\n127\t        if (!server.listening) {\n128\t          resolve();\n129\t          return;\n130\t        }\n131\t        server.close(err => (err ? '/hal';\n\n  const wss = new WebSocketServer({\n    noServer: true,\n    handleProtocols: selectSubprotocol,\n  });\n\n  const handleMessage = createMessageHandler(options.orchestrator);\n  const examplePrompts = options.toolRegistry?.getExamplePrompts() ?? [];\n\n  const deps = {\n    wsAuth: options.wsAuth,\n    sessionStore: options.sessionStore,\n    handleMessage,\n    basePath,\n    onConnect: options.onConnect,\n    onDisconnect: options.onDisconnect,\n    resume: options.resume,\n  };\n\n  const handleUpgrade = createUpgradeHandler(wss, deps);\n  const handleConnection = createConnectionHandler(deps, examplePrompts);\n\n  const server = http.createServer(options.app);\n\n  server.on('upgrade', (request, socket, head) => {\n    handleUpgrade(request, socket, head);\n  });\n\n  wss.on('connection', ws => {\n    handleConnection(ws as ExtWebSocket);\n  });\n\n  const heartbeatMs = options.heartbeatIntervalMs ?? 30_000;\n\n  const heartbeatInterval = setInterval(() => {\n    wss.clients.forEach(ws => {\n      const extWs = ws as ExtWebSocket;\n      if (!extWs.isAlive) {\n        extWs.terminate();\n        return;\n      }\n      extWs.isAlive = false;\n      extWs.ping();\n    });\n  }, heartbeatMs);\n\n  // The listening socket should hold the process open, not the heartbeat: an engine never started must not.\n  heartbeatInterval.unref();\n\n  wss.on('close', () => clearInterval(heartbeatInterval));\n\n  return {\n    server,\n    wss,\n    start: (port?: number) =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        // Refused before the handlers are touched: `listen` throws on a running server, and the swap below stripped its handler.\n        if (server.listening) {\n          reject(new Error('HAL Engine is already started'));\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // ?? not ||, so a configured port 0 means \"let the OS choose\" rather than 8086.\n        const p = port ?? options.port ?? (Number(process.env.PORT) || 8086);\n\n        // Without this an EADDRINUSE settles nothing and takes the process down as an unhandled event.\n        const onError = (error: Error) => reject(error);\n        server.once('error', onError);\n\n        // A listener left from a previous start would report this bind failure as a running server breaking.\n        server.removeListener('error', logLateError);\n\n        server.listen(p, () => {\n          server.removeListener('error', onError);\n\n          // Past the listen window nothing is listening, and an 'error' with no handler ends the process.\n          server.on('error', logLateError);\n\n          // The bound port, not the requested one: a configured 0 means the OS chose it.\n          log.info('server', 'HAL Engine started', {port: boundPort(server, p)});\n          resolve();\n        });\n      }),\n    stop: () =>\n      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n        wss.clients.forEach(ws => ws.close(1001, 'Server shutting down'));\n        wss.close();\n        clearInterval(heartbeatInterval);\n\n        // Nothing to close after a refused start or a second stop: `close` would reject for a server that never ran.\n        if (!server.listening) {\n          resolve();\n          return;\n        }\n        server.close(err => (err ? reject(err) : resolve()));\n      }),\n  };\n}\n\n// A running server's error is a condition to report, not a reason to exit; the consumer decides what to do.\nfunction logLateError(error: Error): void {\n  log.error('server', 'server error after start', {error: error.message});\n}\n\nfunction boundPort(server: http.Server, requested: number): number {\n  const address = server.address();\n  return typeof address === 'object' && address !== null ? address.port : requested;\n}\n","numLines":145,"startLine":1,"totalLines":145}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_f90ac9e213894bdca4ef606a","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

session: T} | {status: 'expired'; userId: string | number} | {status: 'missing'};\n\n/** What a conversation list needs of one session: never its entries, and never its credentials. */\nexport interface SessionSummary {\n  sessionId: string;\n  createdAt: Date;\n  updatedAt: Date;\n  entryCount: number;\n}\n\n/** Where a page of conversations resumes from: the last row the caller saw, which a `SessionSummary` satisfies. */\nexport interface SessionCursor {\n  /** A `Date`, an ISO string or an epoch number, so a row that went through JSON still pages (specs/hal-engine-conversation-list/spec.md). */\n  updatedAt: Date | string | number;\n  sessionId: string;\n}\n\n/** A window onto a user's conversations, newest activity first; `before` pages down that same order. */\nexport interface SessionListOptions {\n  limit?: number;\n  before?: SessionCursor;\n}\n\nexport interface SessionCreateOptions {\n  authHeaders?: ChatSession['authHeaders'];\n  workspaceId?: string | number;\n}\n\nexport interface SessionStore<T extends BaseSession = ChatSession> {\n  create(sessionId: string, userId: string | number, options?: SessionCreateOptions): Awaitable<T>;\n  get(sessionId: string): Awaitable<T | undefined>;\n  delete(sessionId: string): Awaitable<boolean>;\n  count(): Awaitable<number>;\n  clear(): Awaitable<void>;\n  /** Drops a session the client never received, without erasing anything a consumer would want kept. */\n  evict?(sessionId: string): Awaitable<boolean>;\n  /** Write signal: fires once per processed user message; failures are swallowed (specs/hal-engine-session-write-signal/spec.md). */\n  save?(session: T): Awaitable<void>;\n  /** Summaries of the user's conversations, newest activity first, so a client can offer a list of them (specs/hal-engine-conversation-list/spec.md). */\n  listFor?(userId: string | number, options?: SessionListOptions): Awaitable<SessionSummary[]>;\n  /** The user's most recently active session the store still holds, so a connect that names none can continue it (specs/hal-engine-session-resume/spec.md). */\n  latestFor?(userId: string | number): Awaitable<T | undefined>;\n  /** Tells an expired session from a missing one;
